SELECT ALL POINT_TBL.POINT_CODE, POINT_TBL.POINT_NAME
FROM POINT_TBL 
where p_typ = 1 order by POINT_TBL.POINT_CODE

SELECT ALL ITEM_TYPE.T_CODE, ITEM_TYPE.T_NAME
FROM ITEM_TYPE order by ITEM_TYPE.T_CODE

FROM ITEM_TBL

order by ITEM_TBL.I_NAME,ITEM_TBL.I_code
